{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01TPujVF386tir8DLQUFSXqL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016Uybjn3WHp4P4526Wq2mUA","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v5.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nMODELS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models\"\nRESULTS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\nLOG=\"$RESULTS/pure_v5.log\"\n\necho \"=== PURE V5 (hf-mirror) START $(date) ===\" | tee \"$LOG\"\n\n# GLA verified\nSIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/model.safetensors\")\necho \"GLA: OK ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n\n# Download DeltaNet via hf-mirror wget\ndownload_via_mirror() {\n  local REPO_NAME=\"$1\"\n  local LOCAL=\"$2\"\n  local ARCH=\"$3\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    if [ \"$SIZE\" -gt 100000000 ]; then\n      echo \"$ARCH: exists ($SIZE bytes), skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      return 0\n    fi\n  fi\n  \n  mkdir -p \"$LOCAL\"\n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH via hf-mirror START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  \n  # Small files first\n  for f in config.json generation_config.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json special_tokens_map.json; do\n    if [ -f \"$LOCAL/$f\" ]; then\n      continue\n    fi\n    wget --timeout=60 --tries=3 \\\n      \"https://hf-mirror.com/m-a-p/${REPO_NAME}/resolve/main/${f}\" \\\n      -O \"$LOCAL/$f\" 2>&1 | tail -1\n  done\n  \n  # model.safetensors - try hf-mirror first\n  rm -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\"\n  echo \"  Trying hf-mirror for model.safetensors...\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  wget --timeout=600 --tries=2 --continue \\\n    \"https://hf-mirror.com/m-a-p/${REPO_NAME}/resolve/main/model.safetensors\" \\\n    -O \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" 2>&1 | tail -5 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    if [ \"$SIZE\" -gt 100000000 ]; then\n      echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH DONE via hf-mirror: $SIZE bytes $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      return 0\n    fi\n  fi\n  \n  # Fallback: Python with HF_ENDPOINT\n  echo \"  hf-mirror wget failed, trying Python with HF_ENDPOINT...\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  rm -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\"\n  python3 -c \"\nimport os\nos.environ['HF_ENDPOINT'] = 'https://hf-mirror.com'\nos.environ['HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET'] = '1'\nfrom huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download\nhf_hub_download(\n    'm-a-p/$REPO_NAME',\n    'model.safetensors',\n    local_dir='$LOCAL',\n    force_download=True,\n)\nprint('OK via Python hf-mirror')\n\" 2>&1 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH DONE: $SIZE bytes $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH FAILED $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n}\n\ndownload_via_mirror \"340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\" \"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\" \"DeltaNet\"\ndownload_via_mirror \"340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\" \"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\" \"HGRN\"\n\n# ---- VERIFY + EVAL ----\necho \"=== VERIFY ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndeclare -A DIRS\nDIRS[GLA]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nDIRS[DeltaNet]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nDIRS[HGRN]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nALL_OK=true\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"$ARCH: OK ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"$ARCH: MISSING\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    ALL_OK=false\n  fi\ndone\n\necho \"=== EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  OUTDIR=\"$RESULTS/${ARCH}_pure\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    echo \"SKIP EVAL $ARCH: model missing\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python -m lm_eval \\\n    --model hf \\\n    --model_args pretrained=$LOCAL,trust_remote_code=True \\\n    --tasks lambada_openai,arc_easy \\\n    --device cuda:0 \\\n    --batch_size auto \\\n    --log_samples \\\n    --output_path \"$OUTDIR/\" 2>&1 | tail -20 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v5.sh\necho \"OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":556,"cache_read_input_tokens":130307,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":556},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"2c9a29ff-0266-4202-a708-7eb7273656f6"}

The issue is that `lm_eval` doesn't `import fla` before trying to load the model, so transformers doesn't know about these custom model types.\n\n\nThe solution is to ensure fla gets imported before lm_eval runs. The key insight is that importing fla triggers the registration of these custom model types, so I need to ensure that happens before lm_eval tries to load the model.\n\nWriting a Python wrapper...
